## Phase 9 — METHOD CHANGE #3: the pixel-verified loop (2026-11-23)
- Critic round 8 (precision round, sort 0·0, VERDICT: NO) named the same fault family a second time — cropped heads, occluded props, missing subjects, foreign sign text — while the scene-data staging audit read 19/19 PASS. Per RULES §3 the METHOD changed: `tools/audit_pixels.mjs` (+ `tools/pixel_measure.py`) now judges the fleet-probed STILLS. The scene-data audit is a pre-check only; **no frame is called staged until the PIXEL gate passes on its probed still.**
- The pixel gate's three tests: (a) HEAD CROP — the required face's rendered head disc is MEASURED in the still (skin segmentation ringed by the dark outline, scipy components), matched to the projected centre/size, and fails if edge-cut, absent, or ambiguous; (b) FOREGROUND OCCLUSION — screen-space: a named prop's projected legibility rect fails if a closer character disc (calibrated radius) or a closer prop box covers >10% of it (the staging audit's occluder set — buildings + wall + gateleaf — cannot see character-on-prop or prop-on-prop occlusion; at F345/F414 the crane_b box overlaps the calendar's rect 100%); (c) SET BAR — §5 detail/flat measured on the pixels (32×32 blocks, grayscale std; flat = std<5, detail = std≥12), judged per SHOT as §5 writes it (median of the shot's key frames ≥22% detail / ≤78% flat; film median shot ≥28%; ≤40% of shots under).
- CALIBRATION (measured on the chunk2d stills, 2026-11-23): rendered head radius vs the audit's r=0.5 sphere — mayor ≈1.04–1.06× (F669: R 431 vs 414; F687: R 473 vs 448, disc centre (553,30) solved from the chord at y=0 + chin at y≈503); hat-wearing villager/mocker silhouette ≈1.32× (F537: 648×624 px vs the 480 px model disc; face-only ≈1.10×). Occlusion discs use k=1.35 (worst case, hat-inclusive).
- CALIBRATION — centre offsets: the rendered head centre sits far ABOVE the audit's projected centre on close shots (F687 ≈ −623 px, F669 ≈ −576 px, F537 ≈ −342 px vertically). The audit's camera model (approximated lowCams rest; renderer face-limit clamping unmodelled) does not reproduce the render camera's effective aim for close shots — this, not the 5% radius error, is the head-crop mechanism: the sphere test passes a head whose rendered disc is centred ~600 px higher than the model thinks. Hence test (a) measures discs in the image; projected centres are used only as match seeds (tolerance 780 px + size-ratio window 0.45–2.4 + ambiguity = FAIL).
- Gate validation on the unrepaired chunk2d stills: PASS exactly on the frames the critic/driver judged good (F288, F537, F621, F639, F870) and FAIL on every faulted frame, each with its cause named (F219/F241 mayor not identifiable; F345/F414 calendar under crane_b 100%; F474 set 13.7%/85.1%; F570 no disc segments — the back-of-head does not read as skin; F590 set 14.5%/84.4%; F669/F687 skin touches the top edge; F711/F723 blueprint 46% geometric / 74% measured-disc overlap by villager_4; F768/F798/F822 set ~14%/81%). Chunk-scope set bar: median shot detail 26.7% (< 28% film bar), 3/10 shots under.

## Phase 8 — audit-model blind spots (learned from the fleet eye-verdicts, 2026-11-23)
- The staging audit's head sphere (r=0.5) is SMALLER than the rendered head mesh: "head in" passed at F669/F687 while the renders crop the head's top. The audit is a NECESSARY gate, never a sufficient one — fleet stills + the driver's eye + the critic remain the judges.
- The audit does not model FACING/legibility: at F570 the mocker's head passed geometrically (520px) but renders as an illegible back-of-head against the blueprint grid that fills the frame's lower half. A passing subject must also READ.
- Rendered prop clusters can sit outside the audit's prop box: the coins' rendered discs ride ~1 D above the audit's 0.5 D drop box (F590: coins cropped at the frame's top edge, measured y 0–12.4%, while the audit's box read 8/8 corners in). When a prop's visual is a cluster, audit the cluster's bounds, not the origin box.
- Fleet render spec: a single frame is `render:N:N+1` — END must be greater than START (`render:N:N` fails in parseModeSpec). 19 runs were lost to this once; never again.
- Guard flag 3: bundling three one-value fixes into one session is still bundling. One cause, one session, one re-judgement — even when each item is a single constant.

## Phase 10 lessons — 2026-11-23
- SCREEN-Y RESPONSE SIGN (measured twice, both directions): lowering a camera target pitches the camera down and moves frame content UP (screen y decreases) — the audit model's projected head centre moved −744/−789 px for a −0.915 target change on SH09. Luna's SH09 correction assumed the opposite sign and pushed the rendered head from crown-cut to fully out of frame; it was reverted on the fleet evidence. Compute target changes from the measured slope (≈813–862 px per unit on SH09), never from intuition about "aiming lower".
- PIXEL GATE measured-disc FALSE POSITIVE (driver-harness note for the re-derivation spec): the gate's occlusion test (b) flags any segmented skin-toned disc over a prop's rect — the CALENDAR'S OWN cream page segments as a disc (F345: the calendar page itself read as 100% measured-disc overlap while the eye shows it fully legible and geometric overlap is 0%), and on frames flooded with tan wall/ground the segmentation returns one frame-filling "disc" (F345/F687). Judge test (b) by the geometric overlap + the eye when the named occluder is a prop and the "disc" coincides with the prop's own rendered face.
- Pixel gate (driver-authored reference) — three defects found by Astra's review (2026-11-23, phase 11): (1) pixel_measure.py CLAMPS expanded disc bounds to the image, making audit_pixels' "disc crosses edge by >24px" check dead code — crop verdicts actually rest on the skin-touch test alone; preserve UNCLIPPED bounds for the cross check. (2) Pair detection passes on one large segmented region — it must match BOTH named characters' discs before passing a two-face moment. (3) Measured-disc overlap has no depth test — an occluder must be CLOSER (larger/nearer disc or z evidence), not merely overlapping, or background discs false-fail occlusion. Any re-derivation must fix all three and still reproduce the validation table.
- Projected bbox "frame share" from a camera sampler does NOT account for occlusion: F590 round 2's sea-plane share read 23.8% in projection but ~7% in the render (the wall and village occlude most of the plane). Treat projected shares of large background planes as upper bounds, never as the frame's measured composition; the pixel gate on the still is the composition judge.
- Astra batch-2 method adoptions (2026-11-23): (1) STANDING: approve staging with projected evidence, then approve the result with rendered pixels — neither substitutes for the other. (2) An occlusion explanation must account for EVERY hidden glyph: "5M" visible from "15.5M" means "15." is covered, not just "1" — check the kind's source text first (camera moves cannot repair wrong underlying text), then confirm the full inscription on the post-fix still. (3) A head disc out of frame does not prove palm/arm coverage — check the named body part specifically. (4) Occupancy-style measurements use the rendered cluster's aggregate bounds from the sampled eased camera + state at t, never the origin proxy box or the authored TO pose. (5) Composition root causes to name before computing: inverted subject hierarchy (SH04 — protect the lead's screen region), wrong side of the occluding surface (F474 — establishing beats go village-side oblique), uniform-sky allocation (SH11 — layered roofline low, clear corridor high).

// Static staging audit for Fudai Wall, chunk 2. Pure Node; no renderer/deps.
// Geometry uses calibrated head centre y=1.9, r=.5, hip=1.35, top=2.4: phase-6
// engine measurement was ~1.85–1.95; F0 (target 1.55, dist 5.1, fov 34) passes
// and F687 (dist 2.9, fov 30) crops, consistent with this model and not y=1.55.
// Named assumptions: stage-dependent lowCams rests are unavailable, so the
// documented calibrated rest rule below is used. Camera roll is ignored (SH07
// is ≤5°). Face-limit clamping from the renderer is not applied. Box frustum
// corner counts use a world AABB after yaw rotation, per the audit specification.
// D2-CLEAR solids are copied from tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs (town hall, ten village
// houses, three neighbour houses); wall and gateleaf are additional occluders.
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import film from '../scenes/film.js';
import { resolver, resolveTimes, autoMove } from '../lib/film/resolve.js';
import { easeFn } from '../engine/ease.js';
import { lowCams } from '../lib/film/end.js';
import { hh } from '../engine/clips.js';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const words = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'audio/clean.json'), 'utf8'));
const lines = [...new Set(words.map(w => w.line))].map(line => {
  const ws = words.filter(w => w.line === line);
  return { line, start: ws[0].start, end: ws.at(-1).end };
});
const voice = { words, lines };
const R = resolver(voice);
let shots = resolveTimes(film.shots, R, 'shots');
const acts = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(film.acts || {}).map(([id, a]) => [id, resolveTimes(a, R, `acts.${id}`)]));
const props = resolveTimes(film.props || [], R, 'props');
const dur = 68.08;
const cast = film.cast;

// Reconstruct the loader's character/rest inputs from each cast spec. These
// specs share the renderer's proportion fields; hip and head heights stay tied
// to the character's leg, neck, and head dimensions.
const characters = Object.entries(cast).map(([name, c]) => {
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes', c.spec), 'utf8'));
  const spec = { ...raw, id: name };
  const legLen = spec.limbs?.legLen ?? 1.35;
  const hipY = legLen * .93;
  const armLen = spec.limbs?.armLen ?? .95;
  const rest = { hipY, headY: hipY + (spec.body?.neckHeight ?? .35) + (spec.body?.neckToHead ?? .35), neckToHead: spec.body?.neckToHead ?? .35, legLen, armLen,
    ankleY: (spec.limbs?.width ?? .058) / 2 + .005, hipR: [-.22, -.2, 0], hipL: [.22, -.2, 0],
    shoulderR: [-.4, .4, 0], shoulderL: [.4, .4, 0], neck: [0, .4, 0] };
  const timeline = resolveTimes(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(acts[name] || {})), R, `acts.${name}`);
  timeline.root = timeline.root || { x: c.at?.[0] ?? 0, z: c.at?.[1] ?? 0, ry: c.ry ?? 0 };
  timeline.settle ??= true;
  return { name, spec, timeline, rest };
});
const rests = Object.fromEntries(characters.map(ch => [ch.name, ch.rest]));

function posAt(id, t) {
  const c = cast[id], base = c.at, walks = (acts[id]?.walk || []).filter(w => t >= w.t0).sort((a,b)=>a.t0-b.t0);
  if (!walks.length) return [base[0], 0, base[1]];
  const w = walks.at(-1), path = w.path, speed = w.speed || 1;
  let remain = Math.max(0, t - w.t0) * speed;
  for (let i=1;i<path.length;i++) {
    const a=path[i-1], b=path[i], d=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
    if (remain <= d) { const u=d ? remain/d:1; return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*u,0,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*u]; }
    remain -= d;
  }
  const p=path.at(-1); return [p[0],0,p[1]];
}
function faceAt(id,t,p) {
  const a=acts[id]||{}, walks=(a.walk||[]).filter(w=>t>=w.t0);
  if(walks.length){const w=walks.at(-1), path=w.path;let rem=Math.max(0,t-w.t0)*(w.speed||1);
    for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++){const d=Math.hypot(path[i][0]-path[i-1][0],path[i][1]-path[i-1][1]);if(rem<=d)return [path[i][0]-path[i-1][0],path[i][1]-path[i-1][1]];rem-=d;}
    const n=path.length;return [path[n-1][0]-path[n-2][0],path[n-1][1]-path[n-2][1]]; }
  const look=(a.look||[]).filter(k=>k.t<=t).sort((x,y)=>x.t-y.t).at(-1);
  if(look)return [look.at[0]-p[0],look.at[2]-p[2]];
  const ry=(cast[id].ry||0)*Math.PI/180;return [Math.sin(ry),Math.cos(ry)];
}
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const lerp=(a,b,u)=>a+(b-a)*u;
function lerpV(a,b,u){return a.map((v,i)=>lerp(v,b[i],u));}
const raises=[];
const lowered = lowCams(shots, characters, rests, { log: msg => raises.push(msg) });
shots = lowered.shots;
const moved=await autoMove(shots,dur);
const shotById=id=>moved.find(s=>s.id===id);

function camAt(shot,t){const c=shot.camera,a=c.t0??shot.t0,b=c.t1??shot.t1;let u=clamp((t-a)/(b-a));u=easeFn(c.ease??'outCubic')(u);return {pos:lerpV(c.from.pos,c.to.pos,u),target:lerpV(c.from.target,c.to.target,u),fov:lerp(c.from.fov??26,c.to.fov??26,u)};}

function add(a,b){return a.map((x,i)=>x+b[i]);}
function ry(v,yaw){const a=yaw*Math.PI/180;return [v[0]*Math.cos(a)+v[2]*Math.sin(a),v[1],-v[0]*Math.sin(a)+v[2]*Math.cos(a)];}
function box(center,size,yaw=0){const [x,y,z]=size,a=Math.abs(yaw*Math.PI/180);const ex=Math.abs(x*Math.cos(a))+Math.abs(z*Math.sin(a)),ez=Math.abs(x*Math.sin(a))+Math.abs(z*Math.cos(a));return {center,min:[center[0]-ex/2,center[1]-y/2,center[2]-ez/2],max:[center[0]+ex/2,center[1]+y/2,center[2]+ez/2],size:[ex,y,ez]};}
function corners(b){const out=[];for(const x of [b.min[0],b.max[0]])for(const y of [b.min[1],b.max[1]])for(const z of [b.min[2],b.max[2]])out.push([x,y,z]);return out;}
function makeFrame(cam){const f=norm(sub(cam.target,cam.pos)), right=norm(cross(f,[0,1,0])), up=norm(cross(right,f));const v=cam.fov*Math.PI/180,h=2*Math.atan(Math.tan(v/2)*1080/1920);const planes=[];
  for(const [axis,ang,other,swap] of [[right,h/2,up,false],[neg(right),h/2,up,true],[up,v/2,right,true],[neg(up),v/2,right,false]]){const edge=norm(add(f,mul(axis,Math.tan(ang))));let n=norm(swap?cross(other,edge):cross(edge,other));if(dot(n,f)<0)n=neg(n);planes.push({n});}
  planes.push({n:f,dot:0,origin:cam.pos,near:true},{n:neg(f),dot:0,origin:cam.pos,far:true});
  return {cam,planes,point(p){const d=sub(p,cam.pos);return dot(d,f)>=.1&&dot(d,f)<=2000&&planes.slice(0,4).every(pl=>dot(d,pl.n)>=-1e-7);},sphere(p,r){const d=sub(p,cam.pos);return dot(d,f)-r>=.1&&dot(d,f)+r<=2000&&planes.slice(0,4).every(pl=>dot(d,pl.n)>=r-1e-7);},depth(p){return dot(sub(p,cam.pos),f);},height(size,p){const d=this.depth(p);return d>0?2*Math.atan(size/(2*d))/(cam.fov*Math.PI/180)*1920:null;},width(size,p){const d=this.depth(p);return d>0?2*Math.atan(size/(2*d))/(h)*1080:null;},screen(p){const d=sub(p,cam.pos),z=dot(d,f);if(z<=.1)return null;const x=dot(d,right)/(z*Math.tan(h/2)),y=dot(d,up)/(z*Math.tan(v/2));return [(x+1)*540,(1-y)*960];}};
}
const sub=(a,b)=>a.map((v,i)=>v-b[i]), mul=(a,k)=>a.map(v=>v*k), dot=(a,b)=>a.reduce((s,v,i)=>s+v*b[i],0), norm=a=>mul(a,1/(Math.hypot(...a)||1)), cross=(a,b)=>[a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]], neg=a=>mul(a,-1);
function segBox(p0,p1,b){let lo=0,hi=1;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const d=p1[i]-p0[i];if(Math.abs(d)<1e-9){if(p0[i]<b.min[i]||p0[i]>b.max[i])return null;}else{let a=(b.min[i]-p0[i])/d,z=(b.max[i]-p0[i])/d;if(a>z)[a,z]=[z,a];lo=Math.max(lo,a);hi=Math.min(hi,z);if(lo>hi)return null;}}return lo>1e-6&&lo<1-1e-6?lo:null;}
function occluder(p0,p1,t,exclude){return occs(t).filter(o=>o.name!==exclude).map(o=>({name:o.name,k:segBox(p0,p1,o)})).filter(x=>x.k!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.k-b.k)[0]?.name||null;}
const solids=[{name:'town hall',min:[-4.5,0,9.5],max:[4.5,6.4,18.5]}];
for(const [x,z] of [[-16,-3],[-10,-4],[-20,3],[-14,7],[-8,11],[8,-4],[14,-2],[20,3],[14,10],[6,12]])solids.push({name:`village house @${x},${z}`,min:[x-2.45,0,z-2.05],max:[x+2.45,6,z+2.05]});
for(const x of [580,600,620])solids.push({name:`neighbour house @${x}`,min:[x-2.45,0,8-2.05],max:[x+2.45,5.6,8+2.05]});
function occs(t){const out=solids.map(b=>({...b}));out.push({name:'seawall',...box([0,9,-16],[90,18,1.15])});const g=props.find(p=>p.id==='gateleaf');if(g){const st=propState(g,t);if(st.visible)out.push({name:'gateleaf',...box(add(st.at,[0,17.88*(st.lift-.5),0]),[7,17.88,.36])});}return out;}
function propState(p,t){const ks=p.keys||[], before=ks.filter(k=>k.t<=t).sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t), key=before.at(-1)||{};const lastField=f=>before.filter(k=>k[f]!=null).at(-1)?.[f];const visible=key.drop?true:(lastField('in')??p.in??'world')==='world';const at=[...(lastField('at')??p.at)],rot=[...(lastField('rot')??p.rot??[0,0,0])];
  const val=(field,def)=>{if(p[field]==null)return def;const keyed=before.filter(k=>k[field]!=null);if(!keyed.length)return p[field];const prev=keyed.at(-1),ix=keyed.length-1,ease=prev.ease;if(ease!=null&&ix>0){const prior=keyed[ix-1],u=clamp((t-prev.t)/ease);const sm=u*u*(3-2*u);return lerp(prior[field],prev[field],sm);}return prev[field];};
  return {visible,at,rot,page:val('page',0),h:val('h',.8),lift:val('lift',0),lineY:val('lineY',1),pin:val('pin',0),cx:val('cx',0),cy:val('cy',12)};}
function dropState(p,t){const k=(p.keys||[]).filter(k=>k.t<=t).at(-1);if(!k?.drop)return null;const d=k.drop,dt=t-k.t,g=d.g??9.8,v0=d.v0||[0,0,0],from=d.from||k.at||p.at,ground=d.ground??0,a=g/2,c=from[1]-ground,T=a>0?(v0[1]+Math.sqrt(v0[1]**2+4*a*Math.max(0,c)))/(2*a):Infinity;if(d.hide&&dt>T)return null;const u=Math.min(dt,T),y=from[1]+v0[1]*u-.5*g*u*u;return [from[0]+v0[0]*u,y,from[2]+v0[2]*u];}
function boxCheck(frame,b,minPx=0,skipOcc=false,name='subject'){const n=corners(b).filter(p=>frame.point(p)).length,center=frame.point(b.center),px=frame.height(b.size[1],b.center),pw=frame.width(b.size[0],b.center),by=!skipOcc?occluder(frame.cam.pos,b.center,currentTime,name):null,behind=frame.depth(b.center)<=0;return {pass:center&&!by&&px!=null&&px>=minPx&&(n>=4||px>=1920||pw>=1080),n,px,pw,center,occluded:by,behind};}
let currentTime=0;
function propBox(id,t){const p=props.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p)return null;const s=propState(p,t);if(!s.visible)return null;const a=s.at,r=s.rot[1]||0,kind=p.kind;let B,points=[];
  if(kind==='boardsign')B=box(add(a,[0,(p.post?p.leg||1.2:0)+(p.h||.8)/2,0]),[p.w||2,p.h||.8,.12],r);
  if(kind==='calendar')B=box(add(a,[0,1.9,0]),[2.2,3.5,.2],r);
  if(kind==='measure'){if(id.endsWith('label'))return null;}
  if(kind==='money')B=box(add(a,[0,.55,0]),[3.7,1.15,2.1],r);
  if(kind==='crane')B=box(add(a,ry([2,8,0],r)),[11.5,16.8,1.2],r);
  if(kind==='gateleaf')B=box(add(a,[0,17.88*(s.lift-.5),0]),[7,17.88,.36],r);
  if(kind==='seawall')B=box(add(a,[0,9,0]),[90,18,1.15],r);
  if(kind==='blueprint')B=box(add(a,[0,1.7,0]),[3.4,3.4,.2],r);
  if(kind==='orb')return {sphere:add(a,[s.cx,s.cy,0]),r:p.r||1.5,state:s};
  if(kind==='coins'){const q=dropState(p,t);return q?{box:box(q,[.5,.5,.5]),state:s}:null;}
  return B?{box:B,state:s}:null;
}
const houseBoxes=[580,600,620].map(x=>box([x,2.1,8],[4,4.2,3.5]));
const wallBox=box([600,6,-10],[24,12,.8]);
const moments=[
 [219,7.3,'SH04'],[241,8.0333333333,'SH04'],[288,9.6,'SH05'],[345,11.5,'SH05'],[414,13.8,'SH05'],[474,15.8,'SH06'],[537,17.9,'SH07'],[570,19,'SH07'],[590,19.6666667,'SH07B'],[621,20.7,'SH08'],[639,21.3,'SH08'],[669,22.3,'SH09'],[687,22.9,'SH09'],[711,23.7,'SH10'],[723,24.1,'SH10'],[768,25.6,'SH11'],[798,26.6,'SH11'],[822,27.4,'SH11'],[870,29,'SH12']
];
function pointCheck(frame,p){return frame.point(p)&&!occluder(frame.cam.pos,p,currentTime);}
function screenVerticalSpan(frame, points){const ys=points.map(p=>frame.screen(p)).filter(p=>p!==null).map(p=>p[1]);return ys.length?Math.max(...ys)-Math.min(...ys):0;}
function faceCheck(frame,id,t,minPx=0){const p=posAt(id,t),head=[p[0],1.9,p[2]],f=faceAt(id,t,p),to=[frame.cam.pos[0]-p[0],frame.cam.pos[2]-p[2]],angle=Math.acos(clamp(dot(norm(f),norm(to)),-1,1))*180/Math.PI;const visible=frame.sphere(head,.5),occ=occluder(frame.cam.pos,head,t),px=frame.height(1,head);return {pass:angle<=60&&visible&&!occ&&px!=null&&px>=minPx,angle,visible,occ,px};}
const results=[];
for(const [frameNo,t,shotId] of moments){currentTime=t;const shot=shotById(shotId);if(!shot){results.push({frameNo,shotId,checks:['missing shot']});continue;}const cam=camAt(shot,t),fr=makeFrame(cam),checks=[];let occupancy=false;
 if(cam.fov<24||cam.fov>50)checks.push(`fov ${cam.fov.toFixed(2)} out of grammar [24, 50]`);
 const inside=occs(t).find(o=>cam.pos.every((v,i)=>v>=o.min[i]-.3&&v<=o.max[i]+.3));
 if(inside)checks.push(`camera inside ${inside.name}`);
 const prop=(id,minPx,component)=>{let q=propBox(id,t);if(id==='measure_plate'){const p=props.find(x=>x.id==='measure'),s=propState(p,t);q={box:box(add(s.at,[.32,p.full??18,0]),[1.35,.42,.1],s.rot[1]||0),state:s};}
  if(id==='seawall_gate'){const p=props.find(x=>x.id==='seawall'),s=propState(p,t);q={box:box(add(s.at,[0,8.94,.62]),[7,17.88,.55]),state:s};}
  if(!q){checks.push(`${id} absent`);return false;}let ck;if(q.sphere){const inside=fr.sphere(q.sphere,q.r),oc=occluder(cam.pos,q.sphere,t,id),px=fr.height(q.r*2,q.sphere);ck={pass:inside&&!oc&&px!=null&&px>=minPx,px,inside,oc,behind:fr.depth(q.sphere)<=0};}
  else {ck=boxCheck(fr,q.box,minPx,false,id);}
  const by=ck.occluded||ck.oc;checks.push(`${id} ${ck.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${ck.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px${ck.behind?' behind camera':''} corners ${ck.n??'-'}/8${by?` occluded by ${by}`:''}`);if(ck.center&&ck.px>=230)occupancy=true;return ck.pass;};
 const face=(id,minPx=0)=>{const f=faceCheck(fr,id,t,minPx),behind=fr.depth([posAt(id,t)[0],1.9,posAt(id,t)[2]])<=0;checks.push(`${id} face ${f.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} angle ${f.angle.toFixed(1)}° head ${behind?'behind camera':f.visible?'in':'out'} ${f.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px${f.occ?` occluded by ${f.occ}`:''}`);if(f.visible&&f.px>=230)occupancy=true;return f.pass;};
 const body=(id,b)=>{const ck=boxCheck(fr,b,0,true,id);return ck.pass;};
 const bodyIds=ids=>ids.filter(id=>{const p=posAt(id,t),b=box([p[0],1,p[2]],[.9,2,.5]);return body(id,b);});
 const point=(name,p)=>{const pass=pointCheck(fr,p),behind=fr.depth(p)<=0;checks.push(`${name} ${pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} point ${behind?'behind camera':pass?'in':'out'} frustum`);return pass;};
 switch(frameNo){
 case 219: face('mayor');{const n=bodyIds(['villager_1','villager_2','villager_3','villager_4','villager_5','villager_6','mocker_a','mocker_b','mocker_c']).length;checks.push(`BODY ${n>=2?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/2`);}break;
 case 241: face('mayor');prop('popsign',60);{const n=bodyIds(['villager_1','villager_2','villager_3','villager_4','villager_5','villager_6','mocker_a','mocker_b','mocker_c']).length;checks.push(`BODY ${n>=2?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/2`);}break;
 case 288: prop('crane_a',120)||prop('crane_b',120);prop('money',60);prop('moneysign',60);break;
 case 345: prop('calendar',120);checks.push(`calendar effPage ${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='calendar'),t).page.toFixed(1)}${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='calendar'),t).page>=5?' PASS':' FAIL'}`);prop('seawall_gate',200);break;
 case 414: prop('measure_plate',40);checks.push(`measure effH ${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='measure'),t).h.toFixed(1)}${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='measure'),t).h>=17?' PASS':' FAIL'}`);prop('calendar',120);checks.push(`calendar effPage ${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='calendar'),t).page.toFixed(1)}${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='calendar'),t).page>=10?' PASS':' FAIL'}`);checks.push(`gateleaf effLift ${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='gateleaf'),t).lift.toFixed(2)}${propState(props.find(p=>p.id==='gateleaf'),t).lift>=.9?' PASS':' FAIL'}`);break;
 case 474: {const c=boxCheck(fr,wallBox,150,true,'neighbour wall');checks.push(`neighbour wall ${c.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${c.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px corners ${c.n}/8`);if(c.center&&c.px>=230)occupancy=true;prop('sticker_a',60);prop('sticker_b',60);const n=houseBoxes.filter(b=>boxCheck(fr,b,0,true).pass).length;checks.push(`neighbour houses ${n>=1?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/1`);break;}
 case 537: face('mocker_a',200);break;
 case 570: {const p=posAt('mocker_a',t),h=[p[0],1.9,p[2]],headIn=fr.point(h)&&fr.sphere(h,.5),headPx=fr.height(1,h);checks.push(`mocker_a head ${headIn?'PASS':'FAIL'} centre ${fr.depth(h)<=0?'behind camera':fr.point(h)?'in':'out'} frustum ${headPx==null?'n/a':headPx.toFixed(0)+'px'}`);if(headIn&&headPx>=230)occupancy=true;point('wall top',[3,18,-15.42]);break;}
 case 590: {const cp=props.find(p=>p.id==='coins'),dropKey=cp.keys.find(k=>k.drop),coinPos=dropState(cp,t);checks.push(`coins drop-start ${dropKey?.t?.toFixed(3)??'n/a'}s position ${coinPos?`[${coinPos.map(v=>v.toFixed(2)).join(', ')}]`:'hidden'}`);prop('coins',24);point('wall top',[2,18,-15.42]);break;}
 case 621: case 639: {const ids=['mocker_a','mocker_b','mocker_c','villager_2','villager_4'],n=ids.filter(id=>faceCheck(fr,id,t).pass).length;checks.push(`faces ${n>=2?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/2 (angle threshold 60°)`);for(const id of ids){const f=faceCheck(fr,id,t);checks.push(`${id} angle ${f.angle.toFixed(1)}° head ${f.visible?'in':'out'} ${f.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px${f.occ?` occluded by ${f.occ}`:''}`);if(f.pass&&f.px>=230)occupancy=true;}break;}
 case 669: face('mayor',250);break;case 687:face('mayor',200);break;
 case 711: prop('blueprint',150);{const p=props.find(x=>x.id==='blueprint'),s=propState(p,t);point('blueprint line',add(s.at,[.15,.75+s.lineY*1.9,.12]));point('blueprint palm',add(s.at,[1.42,.75+s.lineY*1.9,.13]));}break;
 case 723: prop('blueprint',150);{const p=props.find(x=>x.id==='blueprint'),s=propState(p,t);if(s.pin<1)checks.push(`blueprint ruler FAIL absent effPin ${s.pin}`);else{const b=box(add(s.at,[1.28,1.7,.14]),[.18,2.05,.07]);const q=boxCheck(fr,b,60,false,'blueprint ruler');checks.push(`blueprint ruler ${q.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${q.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px corners ${q.n}/8`);}const l=boxCheck(fr,box(add(s.at,[1.3,2.82,.14]),[.9,.28,.06]),40,false,'blueprint label');checks.push(`blueprint label ${l.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${l.px?.toFixed(0)??'n/a'}px corners ${l.n}/8`);}break;
 case 768: case 798: case 822: prop('seawall',300);{const seaCorners=[[-15,0,-40],[15,0,-40],[-15,0,-20],[15,0,-20]],span=screenVerticalSpan(fr,seaCorners),centreIn=fr.point([0,0,-30]),pass=centreIn&&span>=40;checks.push(`sea patch ${pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${span.toFixed(0)}px centre ${centreIn?'in':'out'}`);const id=frameNo===822?'moonorb':'sunorb';prop(id,0);}break;
 case 870: prop('mocksign',80);{const n=bodyIds(['mocker_b','mocker_c','villager_1','villager_3']).length;checks.push(`passers-by ${n>=2?'PASS':'FAIL'} ${n}/2`);}break;
 }
 if(!occupancy)checks.push('FAIL subjects do not occupy the frame (no required subject ≥230px)');
 results.push({frameNo,t,shotId,checks});
}
if(raises.length){console.log('LOWCAMS raises:');for(const r of raises)console.log(`  ${r}`);}
console.log('FRAME | TIME | SHOT | RESULT | CHECKS');
let failed=0;
for(const r of results){const bad=r.checks.filter(x=>/FAIL| absent|do not occupy|out of grammar|camera inside/.test(x));if(bad.length)failed++;const shot=shotById(r.shotId),cam=shot&&camAt(shot,r.t);if(cam)console.log(`CAM F${r.frameNo} pos [${cam.pos.map(v=>v.toFixed(2)).join(', ')}] target [${cam.target.map(v=>v.toFixed(2)).join(', ')}] fov ${cam.fov.toFixed(2)}`);console.log(`F${r.frameNo} | ${r.t.toFixed(2)} | ${r.shotId} | ${bad.length?'FAIL':'PASS'} | ${r.checks.join('; ')}`);}

// tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs — Seat 1 driver-side static audits for the Fudai build.
// D1: film.props schema (ARRAY of {id, kind, in:'world', at:[x,y,z], keys:[{t, at}]},
//     ids unique, keys non-empty; box entry keeps fetch:true when present),
//     purity scan (no Math.random / no Date in scene sources).
// D2: ONE PLACES table exported from scenes/set.js (all 7 places, at + bounds);
//     every prop `at`, cast `at`, and camera target falls inside a declared
//     place's bounds (zone audit); camera targets sane in y (frustum pre-audit —
//     the looked-at zoom frustum check stays Seat 4's, on the probe stills).
// Run: node tools/audit_d1_d2.mjs   (from the project folder)
// Exit 0 = PASS, 1 = FAIL (failures printed, one per line).
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const fails = [];
const notes = [];
const ok = (cond, msg) => { if (!cond) fails.push(msg); };

const filmSrc = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes/film.js'), 'utf8');
const setSrc = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes/set.js'), 'utf8');

// ---- purity (RULES §4) across all authored scene sources ----
import { readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
const scan = (dir) => {
  for (const e of readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const p = join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) scan(p);
    else if (e.name.endsWith('.js')) {
      const s = readFileSync(p, 'utf8');
      if (/Math\.random/.test(s)) fails.push(`PURITY: Math.random in ${p}`);
      if (/\bDate\s*\(|Date\.now/.test(s)) fails.push(`PURITY: Date in ${p}`);
    }
  }
};
scan(join(ROOT, 'scenes'));

// ---- load the film + the PLACES table ----
const film = (await import(join(ROOT, 'scenes/film.js'))).default;
const setMod = await import(join(ROOT, 'scenes/set.js'));
const PLACES = setMod.PLACES;
ok(PLACES && typeof PLACES === 'object', 'D2: scenes/set.js does not export a PLACES table');
ok(/import\s*\{[^}]*PLACES[^}]*\}\s*from\s*'\.\/set\.js'/.test(filmSrc),
  'D2: film.js does not import PLACES from ./set.js');

const EXPECTED = ['COAST_WALL', 'COAST_VILLAGE', 'SEA', 'NEIGHBOR_COAST', 'FLASHBACK_ROCK', 'ROOM', 'HILL_GRAVE'];
for (const name of EXPECTED) {
  const p = PLACES?.[name];
  ok(p, `D2: PLACES.${name} missing`);
  if (!p) continue;
  ok(Array.isArray(p.at) && p.at.length === 3, `D2: PLACES.${name}.at is not a 3-vector`);
  ok(p.bounds && Array.isArray(p.bounds.x) && Array.isArray(p.bounds.z),
    `D2: PLACES.${name}.bounds missing {x:[..], z:[..]}`);
}
const inBounds = (x, z) => EXPECTED.some(n => {
  const b = PLACES[n]?.bounds; if (!b) return false;
  return x >= b.x[0] && x <= b.x[1] && z >= b.z[0] && z <= b.z[1];
});
const placeOf = (x, z) => EXPECTED.find(n => {
  const b = PLACES[n]?.bounds; if (!b) return false;
  return x >= b.x[0] && x <= b.x[1] && z >= b.z[0] && z <= b.z[1];
});

// ---- D1: props schema ----
ok(Array.isArray(film.props), 'D1: film.props is NOT an array');
if (Array.isArray(film.props)) {
  const ids = new Set();
  for (const p of film.props) {
    ok(p.id && !ids.has(p.id), `D1: prop id missing/duplicated: ${p.id}`);
    ids.add(p.id);
    ok(typeof p.kind === 'string' && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(p.kind), `D1: prop ${p.id}: bad kind ${p.kind}`);
    ok(p.in === 'world', `D1: prop ${p.id}: in !== 'world' (${p.in})`);
    ok(Array.isArray(p.at) && p.at.length === 3 && p.at.every(v => Number.isFinite(v)),
      `D1: prop ${p.id}: at is not a finite 3-vector`);
    ok(Array.isArray(p.keys) && p.keys.length > 0, `D1: prop ${p.id}: keys empty`);
    if (Array.isArray(p.keys)) {
      const nums = p.keys.map(k => k.t).filter(t => typeof t === 'number');
      ok(nums.every((v, i) => i === 0 || v >= nums[i - 1]), `D1: prop ${p.id}: numeric keys not sorted by t`);
      for (const k of p.keys) if (k.at) ok(Array.isArray(k.at) && k.at.length === 3, `D1: prop ${p.id}: a key's at is not a 3-vector`);
    }
    // D2 zone: prop position inside a declared place
    if (Array.isArray(p.at)) ok(inBounds(p.at[0], p.at[2]),
      `D2: prop ${p.id} at [${p.at}] is outside every place's bounds`);
    else notes.push(`prop ${p.id}: no at to zone-check`);
    if (p.id === 'box' || p.kind === 'box') ok(p.fetch === true, 'D1: belongings box entry lost fetch: true');
  }
  if (!film.props.some(p => p.id === 'box' || p.kind === 'box'))
    notes.push('D1: no belongings-box prop yet (expected at opening stage; fetch:true check arms when it lands)');
}

// ---- D2: cast + cameras inside places ----
for (const [id, c] of Object.entries(film.cast || {})) {
  ok(Array.isArray(c.at) && c.at.length === 2, `D2: cast ${id}: at is not [x, z]`);
  if (Array.isArray(c.at)) ok(inBounds(c.at[0], c.at[1]),
    `D2: cast ${id} at [${c.at}] is outside every place's bounds`);
}
ok(Array.isArray(film.shots) && film.shots.length > 0, 'D2: film.shots empty');
for (const [i, s] of (film.shots || []).entries()) {
  const ends = s.camera?.move === 'park' ? [s.camera] : [s.camera?.from, s.camera?.to];
  for (const e of ends) {
    if (!e) { fails.push(`D2: shot ${i + 1}: missing camera from/to`); continue; }
    ok(Array.isArray(e.target) && e.target.length === 3, `D2: shot ${i + 1}: target not a 3-vector`);
    if (Array.isArray(e.target)) {
      ok(inBounds(e.target[0], e.target[2]),
        `D2: shot ${i + 1}: target [${e.target}] outside every place's bounds`);
      ok(e.target[1] >= 0 && e.target[1] <= 19.5,
        `D2: shot ${i + 1}: target y ${e.target[1]} outside sane 0..19.5 (wall is 18 D)`);
    }
    ok(Array.isArray(e.pos) && e.pos.length === 3, `D2: shot ${i + 1}: pos not a 3-vector`);
  }
}

// ---- D2-CLEAR: camera paths vs building solids (phase-7 extension) ----
// Conservative solids from set.js buildWorld at MAXIMUM dimensions (village house
// w<=4.9 -> hx 2.45, d<=4.1 -> hz 2.05, h<=4.65 + roof cone 1.25 -> top 6.0; town hall
// box + cone r4.5 -> x +-4.5, z 9.5..18.5, top 6.4; neighbour houses h 4.2 + roof -> 5.6).
// A path clearing these boxes clears the real buildings. Wall/storeys are NOT solids.

function segHitsBox(p0, p1, b) {
  let t0 = 0, t1 = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const d = p1[i] - p0[i];
    if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-9) { if (p0[i] < b.min[i] || p0[i] > b.max[i]) return false; }
    else {
      let ta = (b.min[i] - p0[i]) / d, tb = (b.max[i] - p0[i]) / d;
      if (ta > tb) { const t = ta; ta = tb; tb = t; }
      t0 = Math.max(t0, ta); t1 = Math.min(t1, tb);
      if (t0 > t1) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

// lab/film/resolve.js: a film file -> the resolved scene the stage draws (2026-11-04, port job). Isomorphic: tools/make_film.mjs runs it in
// node (fs io + puppeteer stages), the app's host page (templates/host/chost.js) runs the SAME code in the page (fetch io + in-page stages).
// It is make_film's resolution moved here unchanged: voice words split into the script lines, every 'line:N...' time, per-character
// timelines (say / listen / blink / settle), hand-overs, sit_on, prop clips, collar tracks, listener gaze, rests, the film end, lowCams,
// autoMove (park drift, push pads, follow + parkAt split), and endFraming.
//
//   const R = await resolveFilm(film, io, opts)  -> { scene, dur, N, fps, W, H, voice, characters, rests, filmProps, END, snaps }
//   io = { log(...a), json(rel, from), text(rel, from), openStage(sceneDef) -> { info, frame(t) -> { views }, close() }, tts?(film),
//          checkVoice?(V), wavOf?(file), rel?(file) }
//        from: 'film' = the film folder (node) / the project root (app), 'root' = the engine root (charsys / the project), 'spec' = a cast
//        spec given as a .json path (charsys root / the project's scenes/)
//   opts = { name, scale (1; .5 = --preview), ownSet: { module, file, label } | null, probe, noDrift, noFacelimit, guardSnaps(characters, props, rests, fps) }
// The resolved.json make_film writes = { ...scene, dur, N, lines: voice.lines }.
import { evalPose } from '../../engine/compose.js';

// ---- own voice file: film.voice = { file, words, lines: [{ who, text }] } (see tools/FILM_API.md); words split across the lines IN ORDER
// (2026-11-05, port job: any language's letters. Accents are folded (NFKD, marks dropped: "canción" = "cancion"), so a film in Spanish,
// German, Russian or Japanese matches its words; ASCII text gives the same tokens as before. Keep in step with tools/make_film.mjs and
// the app's src/shared/trim.ts vtok.)
const vtok = s => String(s).normalize('NFKD').replace(/\p{M}+/gu, '').toLowerCase().replace(/[’']/g, '').replace(/[-–—/]/g, ' ').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N} ]+/gu, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
const TTS_DROPPED = new Set(['pfft', 'tsk', 'tut']);
const JOIN_MAX = 40;   // a line token written without spaces (Japanese, Chinese, Thai) is several transcript words: join up to this many
export function splitOwnWords(words, lines, cast = {}) {
  const ws = words.filter(w => vtok(w.word).length);
  let j = 0; const outW = [], outL = [];
  lines.forEach((L, i) => {
    const n = i + 1, who = L.who != null && L.who in cast ? L.who : null, toks = vtok(L.text || '');
    const mine = [];
    for (const tk of toks) {
      const w = ws[j];
      const got = w ? vtok(w.word).join('') : null;
      if (got === tk) { mine.push(w); j++; continue; }
      if (TTS_DROPPED.has(tk)) continue;
      // a transcript can split / join a word differently (e.g. "o clock" vs "oclock", or a Japanese clause heard as several words):
      // join the next words while they spell the start of the token (two words = the old rule, unchanged)
      if (w && ws[j + 1] && tk.startsWith(got)) {
        let acc = got, k = j + 1, word = w.word;
        while (ws[k] && k - j < JOIN_MAX && acc.length < tk.length) { const nx = acc + vtok(ws[k].word).join(''); if (!tk.startsWith(nx)) break; acc = nx; word += ws[k].word; k++; }
        if (acc === tk) { mine.push({ ...w, word, end: ws[k - 1].end }); j = k; continue; }
      }
      throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: voice file: line ${n} ("${L.text}") expects "${tk}" but words.json has ${w ? `"${w.word}" at ${w.start}s (word ${j + 1})` : 'no more words'}. ${outW.length} words matched before it.`);
    }
    if (!mine.length) throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: voice file: line ${n} ("${L.text}") has no words`);
    for (const w of mine) outW.push({ who, line: n, word: w.word, start: +w.start, end: +w.end });
    outL.push({ line: n, who, text: L.text, start: +mine[0].start, end: +mine[mine.length - 1].end, ...(L.loud ? { loud: true } : {}) });
  });
  if (j < ws.length) throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: voice file: words.json has ${ws.length - j} words after the last line (from "${ws[j].word}" at ${ws[j].start}s): add the missing line(s)`);
  return { words: outW, lines: outL };
}
export async function voiceOf(film, io, log = () => {}) {
  const V = film.voice || { lines: [] };
  if (!V.lines?.length) return { words: [], lines: [], wav: null };
  if (V.file) {
    if (io.checkVoice) io.checkVoice(V);
    if (!V.words) throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: voice.words (word timings json) not found: ${V.words}`);
    let raw = await io.json(V.words, 'film'); if (!Array.isArray(raw)) raw = raw.words || [];
    const { words, lines } = splitOwnWords(raw, V.lines, film.cast || {});
    log(`voice: own file ${io.rel ? io.rel(V.file) : V.file}, ${lines.length} lines, ${words.length} words (${lines.filter(l => !l.who).length} narrator lines)`);
    return { words, lines, wav: io.wavOf ? io.wavOf(V.file) : V.file, own: true };
  }
  if (!io.tts) throw new Error('MAKE_FILM FAILED: the film has no voice.file: give it the recorded voice and its word times (voice: { file, words, lines })');
  return io.tts(film);
}

// ---- 'line:2.word:3+0.1' -> seconds on the film clock (= the voice clock). FX1: any string value starting with 'line:' in acts / props /
// handovers / shots is a time; a time key holding anything else that is not a number is an error naming the film key path.
export const TIME_KEYS = new Set(['t', 't0', 't1', 'parkAt']);
export function resolver(voice) {
  return (ref, where) => {
    if (typeof ref !== 'string') return ref;
    const at = where ? ` at ${where}` : '';
    const m = ref.match(/^line:(\d+)(?:\.(start|end)|\.word:(\d+)(?:\.(start|end))?)?\s*([+-]\s*[\d.]+)?$/);
    if (!m) throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: bad time "${ref}"${at} (seconds, or line:N.start | line:N.end | line:N.word:K[.end] [+-s])`);
    const L = voice.lines.find(l => l.line === +m[1]);
    if (!L) throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: "${ref}"${at}: no line ${m[1]} (the film has ${voice.lines.length} lines)`);
    let t;
    if (m[3]) { const ws = voice.words.filter(w => w.line === +m[1]); const w = ws[+m[3] - 1]; if (!w) throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: "${ref}"${at}: line ${m[1]} has ${ws.length} words`); t = m[4] === 'end' ? w.end : w.start; }
    else t = m[2] === 'end' ? L.end : L.start;
    return +(t + (m[5] ? +m[5].replace(/\s/g, '') : 0)).toFixed(4);
  };
}
const NOTIME = new Set(['text', '_n', '_doc', 'name', 'kind', 'id', 'who', 'owner', 'spec', 'in', 'move', 'ease', 'gait', 'mouth', 'eyes', 'brow']);
export function resolveTimes(x, R, where = '') {
  const isLine = v => typeof v === 'string' && /^\s*line:/.test(v);
  if (Array.isArray(x)) return x.map((v, i) => isLine(v) ? R(v.trim(), `${where}[${i}]`) : resolveTimes(v, R, `${where}[${i}]`));
  if (!x || typeof x !== 'object') return x;
  const o = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(x)) {
    const p = where ? `${where}.${k}` : k;
    if (TIME_KEYS.has(k) && typeof v === 'string') o[k] = R(v.trim(), p);
    else if (TIME_KEYS.has(k) && v != null && typeof v !== 'number' && typeof v !== 'function') throw new Error(`MAKE_FILM FAILED: ${p} = ${JSON.stringify(v)} is not a time (seconds or 'line:N...')`);
    else if (k === 'at' && isLine(v)) o.t0 = R(v.trim(), p);
    else if (k === 'clip') o.name = v;
    else if (isLine(v) && !NOTIME.has(k)) o[k] = R(v.trim(), p);
    else o[k] = resolveTimes(v, R, p);
  }
  return o;
}

// ENG8: a hand-over ENDS the giver's and the taker's handing-arm holds (timeline armEnds); FIX1: the taker carries what they took.
export function handoverArmEnds(handovers, characters) {
  for (const h of handovers || []) {
    if (h.endHolds === false || typeof h.t !== 'number') continue;
    const H = { hold: .2, back: .35, fromSide: 'L', toSide: 'R', ...h };
    for (const [who, side, role] of [[H.from, H.fromSide, 'from'], [H.to, H.toSide, 'to']]) {
      if (h.endHolds && h.endHolds !== true && h.endHolds !== role) continue;
      const c = characters.find(x => x.name === who); if (!c) continue;
      c.timeline.armEnds = [...(c.timeline.armEnds || []), { side, t: +(H.t + H.hold).toFixed(4), since: H.t, out: Math.max(2, Math.round(H.back * 30)), _handover: H.prop }];
      if (role === 'to' && h.carry !== false) c.timeline.clips = [...(c.timeline.clips || []), { name: 'carry', clip: 'carry', t0: +(H.t + H.hold).toFixed(4), params: { prop: H.prop, side }, keep: false }];
    }
  }
}

async function specOf(id, c, io) {
  const s = c.spec ?? id;
  if (typeof s === 'string' && /\.json$/.test(s)) { const j = await io.json(s, 'spec'); return { ...j, id: j.id || id, ...(c.face ? { face: { ...(j.face || {}), ...c.face } } : {}) }; }

export const free = true;
export function build(o = {}) {
  const w=o.w??90,h=o.h??18,gateW=o.gateW??7,g=new THREE.Group();

  const add=(geo,mat,x,y,z,iw=.018)=>{const m=part(geo,mat,iw);m.position.set(x,y,z);g.add(m);return m;};
  add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,h,1.15),concrete,0,h/2,0,.035);
  const panels=Math.floor(w/5);
  for(let i=0;i<panels;i++){const x=-w/2+2.5+i*5;add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.78,h-.25,1.18),panel,x,h/2,.02,.012);}
  add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(gateW,h-.12,.55),steel,0,(h-.12)/2,.62,.025);
  // cross bracing and gate frame
  for(const x of [-gateW/2,gateW/2]) add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.18,h,.7),iron,x,h/2,.56,.018);
  add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(gateW,.18,.7),iron,0,.12,.56,.018);
  for(let y=1.2;y<h-1;y+=2.3){const beam=add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(gateW-.28,.12,.45),iron,0,y,.72,.012);beam.rotation.z=(Math.floor(y/2.3)%2?-.62:.62);}
  // emissive-like lantern boxes at both gate posts
  const glow=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:'#ffd27a'});
  for(const x of [-gateW/2-.45,gateW/2+.45]){add(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.48,.62,.45),iron,x,h*.72,.72,.015);const l=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.28,.36,.3),glow);l.position.set(x,h*.72,.98);g.add(l);}
  return finish(g);
}

const cast = film.cast;

// Reconstruct the loader's character/rest inputs from each cast spec. These
// specs share the renderer's proportion fields; hip and head heights stay tied
// to the character's leg, neck, and head dimensions.
const characters = Object.entries(cast).map(([name, c]) => {
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'scenes', c.spec), 'utf8'));

    shoulderR: [-.4, .4, 0], shoulderL: [.4, .4, 0], neck: [0, .4, 0] };

  timeline.root = timeline.root || { x: c.at?.[0] ?? 0, z: c.at?.[1] ?? 0, ry: c.ry ?? 0 };
  timeline.settle ??= true;
  return { name, spec, timeline, rest };
});

function posAt(id, t) {
  const c = cast[id], base = c.at, walks = (acts[id]?.walk || []).filter(w => t >= w.t0).sort((a,b)=>a.t0-b.t0);
  if (!walks.length) return [base[0], 0, base[1]];
  const w = walks.at(-1), path = w.path, speed = w.speed || 1;
  let remain = Math.max(0, t - w.t0) * speed;
  for (let i=1;i<path.length;i++) {
    const a=path[i-1], b=path[i], d=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);
    if (remain <= d) { const u=d ? remain/d:1; return [a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*u,0,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*u]; }
    remain -= d;
  }
  const p=path.at(-1); return [p[0],0,p[1]];
}
function faceAt(id,t,p) {
  const a=acts[id]||{}, walks=(a.walk||[]).filter(w=>t>=w.t0);

codex
Both family sweeps are running through the same resolver, `lowCams`, and `autoMove` path used by the staging audit. The first pass shows the framing tradeoff is sensitive to aim height: C1 can center the gate/coins but needs its aim lowered to expose sea through the opening; C2 keeps the coin cluster comfortably sized but its current wall-top aim hides the sea plane. I’m widening the sampler ranges and measuring the sea that projects through the actual gate opening.

export const free = true;
export function build(o = {}) {

  const add=(geo,mat,x,y,z,iw=.018)=>{const m=part(geo,mat,iw);m.position.set(x,y,z);g.add(m);return m;};

codex
The sampler found geometrically strong candidates in both families, but checking the actual set mesh revealed a blocking conflict: `scenes/props/seawall.js` builds the seawall as an uninterrupted 90×18 solid box behind the gate. So the C1 “sea through slot” projection is occluded by the wall, and the village-side mid-height C2 samples are also below the wall crest. Because the brief says to apply nothing if neither family meets the constraints, I’m reverting my trial pose and reporting the measured conflict instead of calling projected overlap visible sea.

## Decision

**No family satisfies the brief against the actual scene geometry, so I applied no pose.** I sampled both families at F590 through the pipeline resolver, `lowCams`, and `autoMove`. Their projections contain sea within the gate aperture’s projected bounds, but the seawall mesh is an uninterrupted 90×18 box at the aperture plane. It occludes that sea in the rendered scene. The C2 samples also crop the mocker when the other framing checks are met.

### Best C1 sample

Pose tested: position `add(PLACES.COAST_VILLAGE, [3, 8, 4])` (world `[3, 8, 6]`), target `add(PLACES.COAST_WALL, [0, 10, 0])` (world `[0, 10, -16]`), FOV 50.

At `t=19.667`, the sampled camera was position `[3.36, 7.16, 5.74]`, target `[0.14, 9.64, -16]`, FOV 50.
